# Junk code in ServicioSAP and obsolete statements in the vault — 2026-10-06

> [!abstract] How this was produced
> Read-only sweep of the current code (ServicioSAP `SpExportaEcommerce` 6214ed8 + the uncommitted S1-S4 edits of 2026-10-05) and of the vault. 8 code areas and 6 document groups; every finding was re-checked by a second agent that tried to refute it. Refuted items are removed; `Correction` holds the verifier's fix when the first description was off; `Source = verifier` marks items the verifier found that the first pass missed (checked once). Nothing was edited, compiled or executed. Consolidated view and priorities: [[ESTADO_MIGRACION_TOTAL_2026-10-06]]. Business-rule parity per process: [[PARIDAD_LAN_VS_SERVICIOSAP_2026-10-06]].

**Totals:** 220 junk-code findings (71 DELETE, 51 REPLACE, 85 DECIDE, 13 KEEP) and 215 obsolete statements (33 high, 100 medium, 82 low).

## 1. Junk code by area

Recommendation: **DELETE** = remove (nothing uses it, or it is a test/stub leftover); **REPLACE** = keep the behavior but change the code (duplicate, hard-coded environment, diverges from LAN); **DECIDE** = needs your decision; **KEEP** = looked like junk but must stay.
